SIB, L-The Imperial Bussian Provisional Oivil Administration having opened, on the 1ath August igoo, 4House to collect, under Foreign supervisien, Dues and Likin on Junk Cargoes, I now transmit the statistie ofthat trade from ist April to 3oth June 1902. On all cargoes arriving or leaving by sea-going junks Dues and Duties were collected at Treaty Tarif Idkin at the rate of per cent. ad valorem onjzmports and A per cent ad valornm on Exports, with the ofthe Duties on certain specified staples, wvhich were reduced on ist May igoi to the rate, formerly assessed byth NaivOustom. APPENDIX I. KOWLOON, LAPPA, AND KIAOCHOW: QUARTERLY RETURNS OF TRADE. APIRIL-JUNE 1902.
(Received 19gth July I go 2.) CUSTOM HOUSE, KOWLOON, 15TI JULY 1902. SIR, I.-a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 38 !, Second Series, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade at Kowloon and the neighbouring stations in Native vessels for the second quarter of the year 1902. b. The stations at present controlled by the Kowloon Customs are Taishan (* g), Lintin ( &#39;) and Shamohun ( j), on the north and west; Samun (2 (9), on the east; and Shaiichung () , ) and Shatowkok ( D A), on the Mirs Bay littoral. The Custom House is called L I a E. c. The word &quot;Inwards&quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot; Outwards,&quot; from China. II.
